No unsanitized window on disk:\n5\t//! sanitization is synchronous and always runs before any file write.\n6\t//!\n7\t//! ## Pipeline\n8\t//!\n9\t//! 1. **Keyword pre-filter** — Aho-Corasick scan skips rules with no matching\n10\t//!    keyword in the line (fast path, avoids expensive regex).\n11\t//! 2. **Regex match** — captures the secret candidate (using `secretGroup` to\n12\t//!    identify the capture group, defaulting to group 1 when present).\n13\t//! 3. **Entropy check** — Shannon entropy must meet rule threshold; low-entropy\n14\t//!    strings (placeholders, words) are not redacted.\n15\t//! 4. **Allowlist check** — global and per-rule allowlists suppress known false\n16\t//!    positives; stopwords (≈1480 in vendored config) provide word-level bypass.\n17\t//! 5. **Redact** — replaces matched secret with `[REDACTED:<rule-id>]`.\n18\t//!\n19\t//! ## Known-safe passthrough\n20\t//!\n21\t//! Certain structured fields are never redacted regardless of entropy or regex:\n22\t//! - Bead IDs (`needle-*`)\n23\t//! - The token `[REDACTED:...]` itself (already sanitized output)\n24\t//!\n25\t//! ## Custom patterns\n26\t//!\n27\t//! Workspace-specific rules live in `.needle.yaml` under\n28\t//! `learning.trace_sanitization.custom_patterns`.\n29\t\n30\tuse std::collections::HashSet;\n31\t\n32\tuse aho_corasick::AhoCorasick;\n33\tuse anyhow::{Context, Result};\n34\tuse regex::Regex;\n35\tuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n36\t\n37\t/// Vendored gitleaks config — embedded at compile time.\n38\t///\n39\t/// Update with `needle update-rules`.\n40\tconst GITLEAKS_TOML: &str = include_str!(\"../../config/gitleaks.toml\");\n41\t\n42\t/// Default URL for `needle update-rules`.\n43\tpub const GITLEAKS_UPSTREAM_URL: &str =\n44\t    \"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/gitleaks/gitleaks/main/config/gitleaks.toml\";\n45\t\n46\t// Minimum interesting line length: lines shorter than this can't contain a secret.\n47\tconst MIN_LINE_LEN: usize = 8;\n48\t\n49\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n50\t// Gitleaks TOML schema\n51\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n52\t\n53\t#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]\n54\tstruct GitleaksToml {\n55\t    #[serde(default)]\n56\t    allowlist: Option<GlobalAllowlist>,\n57\t    #[serde(default)]\n58\t    rules: Vec<RuleSpec>,\n59\t}\n60\t\n61\t#[derive(Debug, Deserialize, Default)]\n62\tstruct GlobalAllowlist {\n63\t    #[serde(default)]\n64\t    regexes: Vec<String>,\n65\t    #[serde(default)]\n66\t    stopwords: Vec<String>,\n67\t    // `paths` is only relevant for file scanning (not text sanitization).\n68\t}\n69\t\n70\t#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]\n71\tstruct RuleSpec {\n72\t    id: String,\n73\t    /// Regex pattern for content matching. Rules without `regex` (e.g. path-only\n74\t    /// rules like `pkcs12-file`) are skipped — they are irrelevant for text sanitization.\n75\t    #[serde(default)]\n76\t    regex: Option<String>,\n77\t    #[serde(default)]\n78\t    keywords: Vec<String>,\n79\t    /// Which capture group holds the secret (0 = whole match, 1 = first group).\n80\t    #[serde(rename = \"secretGroup\", default)]\n81\t    secret_group: usize,\n82\t    /// Minimum Shannon entropy required for the secret substring.\n83\t    #[serde(default)]\n84\t    entropy: Option<f64>,\n85\t    #[serde(default)]\n86\t    allowlists: Vec<RuleAllowlist>,\n87\t}\n88\t\n89\t#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]\n90\tstruct RuleAllowlist {\n91\t    #[serde(rename = \"regexTarget\", default)]\n92\t    regex_target: RegexTarget,\n93\t    #[serde(default)]\n94\t    regexes: Vec<String>,\n95\t    #[serde(default)]\n96\t    stopwords: Vec<String>,\n97\t}\n98\t\n99\t/// Which part of the match an allowlist regex is checked against.\n100\t#[derive(Debug, Deserialize, Default, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\n101\t#[serde(rename_all = \"lowercase\")]\n102\tenum RegexTarget {\n103\t    /// Check against the captured secret substring.\n104\t    #[default]\n105\t    Secret,\n106\t    /// Check against the entire regex match.\n107\t    Match,\n108\t    /// Check against the whole input line.\n109\t    Line,\n110\t}\n111\t\n112\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n113\t// Compiled rule\n114\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n115\t\n116\tstruct CompiledRule {\n117\t    id: String,\n118\t    regex: Regex,\n119\t    /// Aho-Corasick automaton for keyword pre-filter (lowercased keywords).\n120\t    /// `None` when the rule has no keywords.\n121\t    keywords: Option<AhoCorasick>,\n122\t    /// Capture group index for the secret value (0 = full match).\n123\t    secret_group: usize,\n124\t    entropy_threshold: Option<f64>,\n125\t    allowlist_regexes: Vec<(Regex, RegexTarget)>,\n126\t    allowlist_stopwords: HashSet<String>,\n127\t}\n128\t\n129\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n130\t// Public API\n131\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n132\t\n133\t/// A workspace-specific custom sanitization pattern.\n134\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n135\tpub struct CustomPattern {\n136\t    /// Rule identifier used in `[REDACTED:<id>]` output.\n137\t    pub id: String,\n138\t    /// Regex pattern. Capture group 1 is the secret; if absent, whole match is used.\n139\t    pub pattern: String,\n140\t    /// Optional minimum Shannon entropy threshold.\n141\t    #[serde(default)]\n142\t    pub entropy: Option<f64>,\n143\t}\n144\t\n145\t/// Sanitizes text content by redacting secrets.\n146\t///\n147\t/// Build once and reuse across traces — rule compilation is expensive.\n148\tpub struct Sanitizer {\n149\t    rules: Vec<CompiledRule>,\n150\t    global_stopwords: HashSet<String>,\n151\t    global_allowlist_regexes: Vec<Regex>,\n152\t    safe_passthrough: AhoCorasick,\n153\t}\n154\t\n155\timpl Sanitizer {\n156\t    /// Build a sanitizer from the vendored gitleaks config and optional custom patterns.\n157\t    pub fn new(custom_patterns: &[CustomPattern]) -> Result<Self> {\n158\t        Self::from_toml(GITLEAKS_TOML, custom_patterns)\n159\t    }\n160\t\n161\t    /// Build a sanitizer from an arbitrary gitleaks TOML string.\n162\t    ///\n163\t    /// Used by `needle update-rules` to validate a downloaded config before\n164\t    /// writing it to disk.\n165\t    pub fn from_toml(toml_str: &str, custom_patterns: &[CustomPattern]) -> Result<Self> {\n166\t        let config: GitleaksToml =\n167\t            toml::from_str(toml_str).context(\"failed to parse gitleaks TOML\")?;\n168\t\n169\t        let global = config.allowlist.unwrap_or_default();\n170\t        let global_stopwords: HashSet<String> = global\n171\t            .stopwords\n172\t            .into_iter()\n173\t            .map(|s| s.to_lowercase())\n174\t            .collect();\n175\t        let global_allowlist_regexes: Vec<Regex> = global\n176\t            .regexes\n177\t            .iter()\n178\t            .filter_map(|r| {\n179\t                let normalized = normalize_regex(r);\n180\t                Regex::new(&normalized)\n181\t                    .map_err(|e| {\n182\t                        tracing::debug!(\n183\t                            rule = \"global-allowlist\",\n184\t                            error = %e,\n185\t                            pattern = %r,\n186\t                            \"skipping invalid allowlist regex\"\n187\t                        );\n188\t                    })\n189\t                    .ok()\n190\t            })\n191\t            .collect();\n192\t\n193\t        let mut rules: Vec<CompiledRule> =\n194\t            Vec::with_capacity(config.rules.len() + custom_patterns.len());\n195\t\n196\t        for spec in &config.rules {\n197\t            match compile_rule(spec) {\n198\t                Some(r) => rules.push(r),\n199\t                None => tracing::debug!(\n200\t                    rule_id = %spec.id,\n201\t                    \"skipping gitleaks rule: regex failed to compile\"\n202\t                ),\n203\t            }\n204\t        }\n205\t\n206\t        for custom in custom_patterns {\n207\t            let normalized = normalize_regex(&custom.pattern);\n208\t            match Regex::new(&normalized) {\n209\t                Ok(regex) => {\n210\t                    // Custom patterns: use capture group 1 when present, else group 0.\n211\t                    let secret_group = if regex.captures_len() > 1 { 1 } else { 0 };\n212\t                    rules.push(CompiledRule {\n213\t                        id: custom.id.clone(),\n214\t                        regex,\n215\t                        keywords: None,\n216\t                        secret_group,\n217\t                        entropy_threshold: custom.entropy,\n218\t                        allowlist_regexes: Vec::new(),\n219\t                        allowlist_stopwords: HashSet::new(),\n220\t                    });\n221\t                }\n222\t                Err(e) => {\n223\t                    tracing::warn!(\n224\t                        pattern_id = %custom.id,\n225\t                        error = %e,\n226\t                        \"custom sanitization pattern failed to compile, skipping\"\n227\t                    );\n228\t                }\n229\t            }\n230\t        }\n231\t\n232\t        // Known-safe passthrough: these substrings in the *secret* portion mean\n233\t        // we skip redaction unconditionally.\n234\t        let safe_passthrough = AhoCorasick::new([\"needle-\", \"[REDACTED:\"])\n235\t            .context(\"failed to build safe-passthrough automaton\")?;\n236\t\n237\t        Ok(Sanitizer {\n238\t            rules,\n239\t            global_stopwords,\n240\t            global_allowlist_regexes,\n241\t            safe_passthrough,\n242\t        })\n243\t    }\n244\t\n245\t    /// Sanitize a string, replacing matched secrets with `[REDACTED:<rule-id>]`.\n246\t    ///\n247\t    /// Runs synchronously — must complete before any write to disk.\n248\t    pub fn sanitize(&self, text: &str) -> String {\n249\t        if text.len() < MIN_LINE_LEN {\n250\t            return text.to_string();\n251\t        }\n252\t\n253\t        let trailing_newline = text.ends_with('\\n');\n254\t        let lines: Vec<&str> = text.lines().collect();\n255\t        let sanitized = lines\n256\t            .iter()\n257\t            .map(|line| self.sanitize_line(line))\n258\t            .collect::<Vec<_>>()\n259\t            .join(\"\\n\");\n260\t\n261\t        if trailing_newline {\n262\t            sanitized + \"\\n\"\n263\t        } else {\n264\t            sanitized\n265\t        }\n266\t    }\n267\t\n268\t    fn sanitize_line(&self, line: &str) -> String {\n269\t        if line.len() < MIN_LINE_LEN {\n270\t            return line.to_string();\n271\t        }\n272\t\n273\t        let mut result = line.to_string();\n274\t        for rule in &self.rules {\n275\t            result = self.apply_rule(rule, &result, line);\n276\t        }\n277\t        result\n278\t    }\n279\t\n280\t    fn apply_rule(&self, rule: &CompiledRule, text: &str, original_line: &str) -> String {\n281\t        // Keyword pre-filter: if the rule declares keywords and none appear in\n282\t        // the lowercased text, skip the regex entirely.\n283\t        if let Some(ref ac) = rule.keywords {\n284\t            let lower = text.to_ascii_lowercase();\n285\t            if !ac.is_match(lower.as_bytes()) {\n286\t                return text.to_string();\n287\t            }\n288\t        }\n289\t\n290\t        let mut result = text.to_string();\n291\t        let mut scan_start = 0usize;\n292\t\n293\t        loop {\n294\t            // Search in the remaining tail of `result`.\n295\t            let haystack = &result[scan_start..];\n296\t            let caps = match rule.regex.captures(haystack) {\n297\t                Some(c) => c,\n298\t                None => break,\n299\t            };\n300\t\n301\t            let full_match = caps.get(0).unwrap();\n302\t\n303\t            // Determine which group holds the secret.\n304\t            let secret_match = if rule.secret_group > 0 && rule.secret_group < caps.len() {\n305\t                caps.get(rule.secret_group)\n306\t            } else if caps.len() > 1 {\n307\t                // Default: use group 1 when available.\n308\t                caps.get(1)\n309\t            } else {\n310\t                caps.get(0)\n311\t            };\n312\t\n313\t            let secret_m = match secret_match {\n314\t                Some(m) => m,\n315\t                None => {\n316\t                    scan_start += full_match.end();\n317\t                    continue;\n318\t                }\n319\t            };\n320\t\n321\t            let secret_str = secret_m.as_str();\n322\t\n323\t            // Entropy gate: skip low-entropy strings (placeholders, env var names).\n324\t            if let Some(threshold) = rule.entropy_threshold {\n325\t                if shannon_entropy(secret_str) < threshold {\n326\t                    scan_start += full_match.end();\n327\t                    continue;\n328\t                }\n329\t            }\n330\t\n331\t            // Global stopwords (lowercased comparison).\n332\t            let secret_lower = secret_str.to_lowercase();\n333\t            if self.global_stopwords.contains(&secret_lower) {\n334\t                scan_start += full_match.end();\n335\t                continue;\n336\t            }\n337\t\n338\t            // Global allowlist regexes checked against the secret.\n339\t            if self\n340\t                .global_allowlist_regexes\n341\t                .iter()\n342\t                .any(|r| r.is_match(secret_str))\n343\t            {\n344\t                scan_start += full_match.end();\n345\t                continue;\n346\t            }\n347\t\n348\t            // Known-safe passthrough: bead IDs, already-redacted strings.\n349\t            if self.safe_passthrough.is_match(secret_str.as_bytes()) {\n350\t                scan_start += full_match.end();\n351\t                continue;\n352\t            }\n353\t\n354\t            // Per-rule allowlist checks.\n355\t            let mut allowed = false;\n356\t            for (al_re, target) in &rule.allowlist_regexes {\n357\t                let subject = match target {\n358\t                    RegexTarget::Secret => secret_str,\n359\t                    RegexTarget::Match => full_match.as_str(),\n360\t                    RegexTarget::Line => original_line,\n361\t                };\n362\t                if al_re.is_match(subject) {\n363\t                    allowed = true;\n364\t                    break;\n365\t                }\n366\t            }\n367\t            if allowed {\n368\t                scan_start += full_match.end();\n369\t                continue;\n370\t            }\n371\t\n372\t            // Per-rule stopwords.\n373\t            if rule.allowlist_stopwords.contains(&secret_lower) {\n374\t                scan_start += full_match.end();\n375\t                continue;\n376\t            }\n377\t\n378\t            // Redact: replace only the secret group span with [REDACTED:<id>].\n379\t            let redaction = format!(\"[REDACTED:{}]\", rule.id);\n380\t\n381\t            let abs_secret_start = scan_start + secret_m.start();\n382\t            let abs_secret_end = scan_start + secret_m.end();\n383\t            let abs_full_end = scan_start + full_match.end();\n384\t\n385\t            result = format!(\n386\t                \"{}{}{}\",\n387\t                &result[..abs_secret_start],\n388\t                redaction,\n389\t                &result[abs_secret_end..]\n390\t            );\n391\t\n392\t            // Advance past the (now possibly length-changed) full match.\n393\t            let delta = redaction.len() as isize - (abs_secret_end - abs_secret_start) as isize;\n394\t            scan_start = ((abs_full_end as isize) + delta) as usize;\n395\t        }\n396\t\n397\t        result\n398\t    }\n399\t\n400\t    /// Number of successfully compiled rules (gitleaks + custom).\n401\t    pub fn rule_count(&self) -> usize {\n402\t        self.rules.len()\n403\t    }\n404\t\n405\t    /// Returns the embedded vendored gitleaks TOML text.\n406\t    pub fn vendored_toml() -> &'static str {\n407\t        GITLEAKS_TOML\n408\t    }\n409\t}\n410\t\n411\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n412\t// Helpers\n413\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n414\t\n415\tfn compile_rule(spec: &RuleSpec) -> Option<CompiledRule> {\n416\t    // Path-only rules (e.g. pkcs12-file) have no regex — skip them silently.\n417\t    let raw_regex = spec.regex.as_deref()?;\n418\t    let normalized = normalize_regex(raw_regex);\n419\t    let regex = Regex::new(&normalized)\n420\t        .map_err(|e| {\n421\t            tracing::debug!(\n422\t                rule_id = %spec.id,\n423\t                error = %e,\n424\t                pattern = %raw_regex,\n425\t                \"gitleaks rule regex compile error\"\n426\t            );\n427\t        })\n428\t        .ok()?;\n429\t\n430\t    let keywords = if spec.keywords.is_empty() {\n431\t        None\n432\t    } else {\n433\t        let lower_kw: Vec<String> = spec.keywords.iter().map(|k| k.to_lowercase()).collect();\n434\t        AhoCorasick::new(&lower_kw)\n435\t            .map_err(|e| {\n436\t                tracing::debug!(\n437\t                    rule_id = %spec.id,\n438\t                    error = %e,\n439\t                    \"failed to build keyword automaton\"\n440\t                );\n441\t            })\n442\t            .ok()\n443\t    };\n444\t\n445\t    let mut allowlist_regexes: Vec<(Regex, RegexTarget)> = Vec::new();\n446\t    let mut allowlist_stopwords: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();\n447\t\n448\t    for al in &spec.allowlists {\n449\t        for re_str in &al.regexes {\n450\t            let normalized = normalize_regex(re_str);\n451\t            match Regex::new(&normalized) {\n452\t                Ok(re) => allowlist_regexes.push((re, al.regex_target)),\n453\t                Err(e) => tracing::debug!(\n454\t                    rule_id = %spec.id,\n455\t                    error = %e,\n456\t                    pattern = %re_str,\n457\t                    \"skipping invalid allowlist regex\"\n458\t                ),\n459\t            }\n460\t        }\n461\t        for sw in &al.stopwords {\n462\t            allowlist_stopwords.insert(sw.to_lowercase());\n463\t        }\n464\t    }\n465\t\n466\t    Some(CompiledRule {\n467\t        id: spec.id.clone(),\n468\t        regex,\n469\t        keywords,\n470\t        secret_group: spec.secret_group,\n471\t        entropy_threshold: spec.entropy,\n472\t        allowlist_regexes,\n473\t        allowlist_stopwords,\n474\t    })\n475\t}\n476\t\n477\t/// Normalize a gitleaks regex for compatibility with the Rust `regex` crate.\n478\t///\n479\t/// Converts POSIX character class syntax (`[[:alnum:]]` etc.) to equivalent\n480\t/// Rust regex syntax, which is RE2-based and does not support POSIX classes.\n481\tfn normalize_regex(pattern: &str) -> String {\n482\t    pattern\n483\t        .replace(\"[[:alnum:]]\", \"[a-zA-Z0-9]\")\n484\t        .replace(\"[[:alpha:]]\", \"[a-zA-Z]\")\n485\t        .replace(\"[[:digit:]]\", \"[0-9]\")\n486\t        .replace(\"[[:lower:]]\", \"[a-z]\")\n487\t        .replace(\"[[:upper:]]\", \"[A-Z]\")\n488\t        .replace(\"[[:space:]]\", r\"[\\t\\n\\r ]\")\n489\t        .replace(\"[[:print:]]\", r\"[\\x20-\\x7e]\")\n490\t        .replace(\"[[:ascii:]]\", r\"[\\x00-\\x7f]\")\n491\t}\n492\t\n493\t/// Calculate Shannon entropy of a string (base-2, over byte values).\n494\t///\n495\t/// Used to distinguish high-entropy secrets from low-entropy placeholders.\n496\tfn shannon_entropy(s: &str) -> f64 {\n497\t    if s.is_empty() {\n498\t        return 0.0;\n499\t    }\n500\t    let mut counts = [0u32; 256];\n501\t    for byte in s.bytes() {\n502\t        counts[byte as usize] += 1;\n503\t    }\n504\t    let len = s.len() as f64;\n505\t    counts\n506\t        .iter()\n507\t        .filter(|&&c| c > 0)\n508\t        .map(|&c| {\n509\t            let p = c as f64 / len;\n510\t            -p * p.log2()\n511\t        })\n512\t        .sum()\n513\t}\n514\t\n515\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n516\t// Tests\n517\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n518\t\n519\t#[cfg(test)]\n520\tmod tests {\n521\t    use super::*;\n522\t\n523\t    fn make_sanitizer() -> Sanitizer {\n524\t        Sanitizer::new(&[]).expect(\"failed to build sanitizer from vendored config\")\n525\t    }\n526\t\n527\t    #[test]\n528\t    fn sanitizer_builds_from_vendored_toml() {\n529\t        let s = make_sanitizer();\n530\t        // Vendored config has 222 rules; allow for minor variation if updated.\n531\t        assert!(\n532\t            s.rule_count() >= 200,\n533\t            \"expected >= 200 compiled rules, got {}\",\n534\t            s.rule_count()\n535\t        );\n536\t    }\n537\t\n538\t    #[test]\n539\t    fn sanitizer_redacts_anthropic_api_key() {\n540\t        let s = make_sanitizer();\n541\t        // Anthropic API key format: sk-ant-api03-<93 chars>AA\n542\t        let fake_key = format!(\n543\t            \"sk-ant-api03-{:0>93}AA\",\n544\t            \"ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789_-ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTU\"\n545\t        );\n546\t        let line = format!(\"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY={}\", fake_key);\n547\t        let result = s.sanitize(&line);\n548\t        assert!(\n549\t            result.contains(\"[REDACTED:anthropic-api-key]\"),\n550\t            \"expected redaction, got: {}\",\n551\t            result\n552\t        );\n553\t        assert!(!result.contains(&fake_key), \"key should be redacted\");\n554\t    }\n555\t\n556\t    #[test]\n557\t    fn sanitizer_redacts_gcp_api_key() {\n558\t        let s = make_sanitizer();\n559\t        // GCP API key: AIza + 35 alphanumeric/dash chars (high entropy)\n560\t        let fake_key = \"AIzaSyBnFb9RkQ3mD2eWl8TpXa0vN7hJcK4oMiY\";\n561\t        let line = format!(\"key = \\\"{}\\\"\", fake_key);\n562\t        let result = s.sanitize(&line);\n563\t        assert!(\n564\t            result.contains(\"[REDACTED:gcp-api-key]\"),\n565\t            \"expected gcp-api-key redaction, got: {}\",\n566\t            result\n567\t        );\n568\t    }\n569\t\n570\t    #[test]\n571\t    fn sanitizer_preserves_bead_ids() {\n572\t        let s = make_sanitizer();\n573\t        // Bead IDs must never be redacted.\n574\t        let line = \"processing bead needle-wysd.2.2 in workspace\";\n575\t        let result = s.sanitize(line);\n576\t        assert_eq!(result, line, \"bead ID should not be redacted\");\n577\t    }\n578\t\n579\t    #[test]\n580\t    fn sanitizer_preserves_already_redacted() {\n581\t        let s = make_sanitizer();\n582\t        let line = \"token=[REDACTED:anthropic-api-key] was sanitized\";\n583\t        let result = s.sanitize(line);\n584\t        assert_eq!(result, line, \"already-redacted token should pass through\");\n585\t    }\n586\t\n587\t    #[test]\n588\t    fn sanitizer_handles_low_entropy_placeholder() {\n589\t        let s = make_sanitizer();\n590\t        // A placeholder like \"AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA\" has zero entropy and should\n591\t        // not be redacted even if it looks like an API key.\n592\t        let line = \"AIzaAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA\";\n593\t        let result = s.sanitize(line);\n594\t        // Low entropy — should not be redacted.\n595\t        assert!(\n596\t            !result.contains(\"[REDACTED:\"),\n597\t            \"low-entropy placeholder should not be redacted\"\n598\t        );\n599\t    }\n600\t\n601\t    #[test]\n602\t    fn sanitizer_with_custom_pattern() {\n603\t        let custom = vec![CustomPattern {\n604\t Trace sanitization: gitleaks-based secret redaction.\n//!\n//! Sanitizes trace content before writing to disk by applying vendored gitleaks\n//! rules and workspace-specific custom patterns. No unsanitized window on disk:\n//! sanitization is synchronous and always runs before any file write.\n//!\n//! ## Pipeline\n//!\n//! 1. **Keyword pre-filter** — Aho-Corasick scan skips rules with no matching\n//!    keyword in the line (fast path, avoids expensive regex).\n//! 2. **Regex match** — captures the secret candidate (using `secretGroup` to\n//!    identify the capture group, defaulting to group 1 when present).\n//! 3. **Entropy check** — Shannon entropy must meet rule threshold; low-entropy\n//!    strings (placeholders, words) are not redacted.\n//! 4. **Allowlist check** — global and per-rule allowlists suppress known false\n//!    positives; stopwords (≈1480 in vendored config) provide word-level bypass.\n//! 5. **Redact** — replaces matched secret with `[REDACTED:<rule-id>]`.\n//!\n//! ## Known-safe passthrough\n//!\n//! Certain structured fields are never redacted regardless of entropy or regex:\n//! - Bead IDs (`needle-*`)\n//! - The token `[REDACTED:...]` itself (already sanitized output)\n//!\n//! ## Custom patterns\n//!\n//! Workspace-specific rules live in `.needle.yaml` under\n//! `learning.trace_sanitization.custom_patterns`.\n\nuse std::collections::HashSet;\n\nuse aho_corasick::AhoCorasick;\nuse anyhow::{Context, Result};\nuse regex::Regex;\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n\n/// Vendored gitleaks config — embedded at compile time.\n///\n/// Update with `needle update-rules`.\nconst GITLEAKS_TOML: &str = include_str!(\"../../config/gitleaks.toml\");\n\n/// Default URL for `needle update-rules`.\npub const GITLEAKS_UPSTREAM_URL: &str =\n    \"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/gitleaks/gitleaks/main/config/gitleaks.toml\";\n\n// Minimum interesting line length: lines shorter than this can't contain a secret.\nconst MIN_LINE_LEN: usize = 8;\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Gitleaks TOML schema\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]\nstruct GitleaksToml {\n    #[serde(default)]\n    allowlist: Option<GlobalAllowlist>,\n    #[serde(default)]\n    rules: Vec<RuleSpec>,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Deserialize, Default)]\nstruct GlobalAllowlist {\n    #[serde(default)]\n    regexes: Vec<String>,\n    #[serde(default)]\n    stopwords: Vec<String>,\n    // `paths` is only relevant for file scanning (not text sanitization).\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]\nstruct RuleSpec {\n    id: String,\n    /// Regex pattern for content matching. Rules without `regex` (e.g. path-only\n    /// rules like `pkcs12-file`) are skipped — they are irrelevant for text sanitization.\n    #[serde(default)]\n    regex: Option<String>,\n    #[serde(default)]\n    keywords: Vec<String>,\n    /// Which capture group holds the secret (0 = whole match, 1 = first group).\n    #[serde(rename = \"secretGroup\", default)]\n    secret_group: usize,\n    /// Minimum Shannon entropy required for the secret substring.\n    #[serde(default)]\n    entropy: Option<f64>,\n    #[serde(default)]\n    allowlists: Vec<RuleAllowlist>,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]\nstruct RuleAllowlist {\n    #[serde(rename = \"regexTarget\", default)]\n    regex_target: RegexTarget,\n    #[serde(default)]\n    regexes: Vec<String>,\n    #[serde(default)]\n    stopwords: Vec<String>,\n}\n\n/// Which part of the match an allowlist regex is checked against.\n#[derive(Debug, Deserialize, Default, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\n#[serde(rename_all = \"lowercase\")]\nenum RegexTarget {\n    /// Check against the captured secret substring.\n    #[default]\n    Secret,\n    /// Check against the entire regex match.\n    Match,\n    /// Check against the whole input line.\n    Line,\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Compiled rule\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nstruct CompiledRule {\n    id: String,\n    regex: Regex,\n    /// Aho-Corasick automaton for keyword pre-filter (lowercased keywords).\n    /// `None` when the rule has no keywords.\n    keywords: Option<AhoCorasick>,\n    /// Capture group index for the secret value (0 = full match).\n    secret_group: usize,\n    entropy_threshold: Option<f64>,\n    allowlist_regexes: Vec<(Regex, RegexTarget)>,\n    allowlist_stopwords: HashSet<String>,\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Public API\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// A workspace-specific custom sanitization pattern.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct CustomPattern {\n    /// Rule identifier used in `[REDACTED:<id>]` output.\n    pub id: String,\n    /// Regex pattern. Capture group 1 is the secret; if absent, whole match is used.\n    pub pattern: String,\n    /// Optional minimum Shannon entropy threshold.\n    #[serde(default)]\n    pub entropy: Option<f64>,\n}\n\n/// Sanitizes text content by redacting secrets.\n///\n/// Build once and reuse across traces — rule compilation is expensive.\npub struct Sanitizer {\n    rules: Vec<CompiledRule>,\n    global_stopwords: HashSet<String>,\n    global_allowlist_regexes: Vec<Regex>,\n    safe_passthrough: AhoCorasick,\n}\n\nimpl Sanitizer {\n    /// Build a sanitizer from the vendored gitleaks config and optional custom patterns.\n    pub fn new(custom_patterns: &[CustomPattern]) -> Result<Self> {\n        Self::from_toml(GITLEAKS_TOML, custom_patterns)\n    }\n\n    /// Build a sanitizer from an arbitrary gitleaks TOML string.\n    ///\n    /// Used by `needle update-rules` to validate a downloaded config before\n    /// writing it to disk.\n    pub fn from_toml(toml_str: &str, custom_patterns: &[CustomPattern]) -> Result<Self> {\n        let config: GitleaksToml =\n            toml::from_str(toml_str).context(\"failed to parse gitleaks TOML\")?;\n\n        let global = config.allowlist.unwrap_or_default();\n        let global_stopwords: HashSet<String> = global\n            .stopwords\n            .into_iter()\n            .map(|s| s.to_lowercase())\n            .collect();\n        let global_allowlist_regexes: Vec<Regex> = global\n            .regexes\n            .iter()\n            .filter_map(|r| {\n                let normalized = normalize_regex(r);\n                Regex::new(&normalized)\n                    .map_err(|e| {\n                        tracing::debug!(\n                            rule = \"global-allowlist\",\n                            error = %e,\n                            pattern = %r,\n                            \"skipping invalid allowlist regex\"\n                        );\n                    })\n                    .ok()\n            })\n            .collect();\n\n        let mut rules: Vec<CompiledRule> =\n            Vec::with_capacity(config.rules.len() + custom_patterns.len());\n\n        for spec in &config.rules {\n            match compile_rule(spec) {\n                Some(r) => rules.push(r),\n                None => tracing::debug!(\n                    rule_id = %spec.id,\n                    \"skipping gitleaks rule: regex failed to compile\"\n                ),\n            }\n        }\n\n        for custom in custom_patterns {\n            let normalized = normalize_regex(&custom.pattern);\n            match Regex::new(&normalized) {\n                Ok(regex) => {\n                    // Custom patterns: use capture group 1 when present, else group 0.\n                    let secret_group = if regex.captures_len() > 1 { 1 } else { 0 };\n                    rules.push(CompiledRule {\n                        id: custom.id.clone(),\n                        regex,\n                        keywords: None,\n                        secret_group,\n                        entropy_threshold: custom.entropy,\n                        allowlist_regexes: Vec::new(),\n                        allowlist_stopwords: HashSet::new(),\n                    });\n                }\n                Err(e) => {\n                    tracing::warn!(\n                        pattern_id = %custom.id,\n                        error = %e,\n                        \"custom sanitization pattern failed to compile, skipping\"\n                    );\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Known-safe passthrough: these substrings in the *secret* portion mean\n        // we skip redaction unconditionally.\n        let safe_passthrough = AhoCorasick::new([\"needle-\", \"[REDACTED:\"])\n            .context(\"failed to build safe-passthrough automaton\")?;\n\n        Ok(Sanitizer {\n            rules,\n            global_stopwords,\n            global_allowlist_regexes,\n            safe_passthrough,\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Sanitize a string, replacing matched secrets with `[REDACTED:<rule-id>]`.\n    ///\n    /// Runs synchronously — must complete before any write to disk.\n    pub fn sanitize(&self, text: &str) -> String {\n        if text.len() < MIN_LINE_LEN {\n            return text.to_string();\n        }\n\n        let trailing_newline = text.ends_with('\\n');\n        let lines: Vec<&str> = text.lines().collect();\n        let sanitized = lines\n            .iter()\n            .map(|line| self.sanitize_line(line))\n            .collect::<Vec<_>>()\n            .join(\"\\n\");\n\n        if trailing_newline {\n            sanitized + \"\\n\"\n        } else {\n            sanitized\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn sanitize_line(&self, line: &str) -> String {\n        if line.len() < MIN_LINE_LEN {\n            return line.to_string();\n        }\n\n        let mut result = line.to_string();\n        for rule in &self.rules {\n            result = self.apply_rule(rule, &result, line);\n        }\n        result\n    }\n\n    fn apply_rule(&self, rule: &CompiledRule, text: &str, original_line: &str) -> String {\n        // Keyword pre-filter: if the rule declares keywords and none appear in\n        // the lowercased text, skip the regex entirely.\n        if let Some(ref ac) = rule.keywords {\n            let lower = text.to_ascii_lowercase();\n            if !ac.is_match(lower.as_bytes()) {\n                return text.to_string();\n            }\n        }\n\n        let mut result = text.to_string();\n        let mut scan_start = 0usize;\n\n        loop {\n            // Search in the remaining tail of `result`.\n            let haystack = &result[scan_start..];\n            let caps = match rule.regex.captures(haystack) {\n                Some(c) => c,\n                None => break,\n            };\n\n            let full_match = caps.get(0).unwrap();\n\n            // Determine which group holds the secret.\n            let secret_match = if rule.secret_group > 0 && rule.secret_group < caps.len() {\n                caps.get(rule.secret_group)\n            } else if caps.len() > 1 {\n                // Default: use group 1 when available.\n                caps.get(1)\n            } else {\n                caps.get(0)\n            };\n\n            let secret_m = match secret_match {\n                Some(m) => m,\n                None => {\n                    scan_start += full_match.end();\n                    continue;\n                }\n            };\n\n            let secret_str = secret_m.as_str();\n\n            // Entropy gate: skip low-entropy strings (placeholders, env var names).\n            if let Some(threshold) = rule.entropy_threshold {\n                if shannon_entropy(secret_str) < threshold {\n                    scan_start += full_match.end();\n                    continue;\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Global stopwords (lowercased comparison).\n            let secret_lower = secret_str.to_lowercase();\n            if self.global_stopwords.contains(&secret_lower) {\n                scan_start += full_match.end();\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            // Global allowlist regexes checked against the secret.\n            if self\n                .global_allowlist_regexes\n                .iter()\n                .any(|r| r.is_match(secret_str))\n            {\n                scan_start += full_match.end();\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            // Known-safe passthrough: bead IDs, already-redacted strings.\n            if self.safe_passthrough.is_match(secret_str.as_bytes()) {\n                scan_start += full_match.end();\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            // Per-rule allowlist checks.\n            let mut allowed = false;\n            for (al_re, target) in &rule.allowlist_regexes {\n                let subject = match target {\n                    RegexTarget::Secret => secret_str,\n                    RegexTarget::Match => full_match.as_str(),\n                    RegexTarget::Line => original_line,\n                };\n                if al_re.is_match(subject) {\n                    allowed = true;\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n            if allowed {\n                scan_start += full_match.end();\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            // Per-rule stopwords.\n            if rule.allowlist_stopwords.contains(&secret_lower) {\n                scan_start += full_match.end();\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            // Redact: replace only the secret group span with [REDACTED:<id>].\n            let redaction = format!(\"[REDACTED:{}]\", rule.id);\n\n            let abs_secret_start = scan_start + secret_m.start();\n            let abs_secret_end = scan_start + secret_m.end();\n            let abs_full_end = scan_start + full_match.end();\n\n            result = format!(\n                \"{}{}{}\",\n                &result[..abs_secret_start],\n                redaction,\n                &result[abs_secret_end..]\n            );\n\n            // Advance past the (now possibly length-changed) full match.\n            let delta = redaction.len() as isize - (abs_secret_end - abs_secret_start) as isize;\n            scan_start = ((abs_full_end as isize) + delta) as usize;\n        }\n\n        result\n    }\n\n    /// Number of successfully compiled rules (gitleaks + custom).\n    pub fn rule_count(&self) -> usize {\n        self.rules.len()\n    }\n\n    /// Returns the embedded vendored gitleaks TOML text.\n    pub fn vendored_toml() -> &'static str {\n        GITLEAKS_TOML\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Helpers\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nfn compile_rule(spec: &RuleSpec) -> Option<CompiledRule> {\n    // Path-only rules (e.g. pkcs12-file) have no regex — skip them silently.\n    let raw_regex = spec.regex.as_deref()?;\n    let normalized = normalize_regex(raw_regex);\n    let regex = Regex::new(&normalized)\n        .map_err(|e| {\n            tracing::debug!(\n                rule_id = %spec.id,\n                error = %e,\n                pattern = %raw_regex,\n                \"gitleaks rule regex compile error\"\n            );\n        })\n        .ok()?;\n\n    let keywords = if spec.keywords.is_empty() {\n        None\n    } else {\n        let lower_kw: Vec<String> = spec.keywords.iter().map(|k| k.to_lowercase()).collect();\n        AhoCorasick::new(&lower_kw)\n            .map_err(|e| {\n                tracing::debug!(\n                    rule_id = %spec.id,\n                    error = %e,\n                    \"failed to build keyword automaton\"\n                );\n            })\n            .ok()\n    };\n\n    let mut allowlist_regexes: Vec<(Regex, RegexTarget)> = Vec::new();\n    let mut allowlist_stopwords: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();\n\n    for al in &spec.allowlists {\n        for re_str in &al.regexes {\n            let normalized = normalize_regex(re_str);\n            match Regex::new(&normalized) {\n                Ok(re) => allowlist_regexes.push((re, al.regex_target)),\n                Err(e) => tracing::debug!(\n                    rule_id = %spec.id,\n                    error = %e,\n                    pattern = %re_str,\n                    \"skipping invalid allowlist regex\"\n                ),\n            }\n        }\n        for sw in &al.stopwords {\n            allowlist_stopwords.insert(sw.to_lowercase());\n        }\n    }\n\n    Some(CompiledRule {\n        id: spec.id.clone(),\n        regex,\n        keywords,\n        secret_group: spec.secret_group,\n        entropy_threshold: spec.entropy,\n        allowlist_regexes,\n        allowlist_stopwords,\n    })\n}\n\n/// Normalize a gitleaks regex for compatibility with the Rust `regex` crate.\n///\n/// Converts POSIX character class syntax (`[[:alnum:]]` etc.) to equivalent\n/// Rust regex syntax, which is RE2-based and does not support POSIX classes.\nfn normalize_regex(pattern: &str) -> String {\n    pattern\n        .replace(\"[[:alnum:]]\", \"[a-zA-Z0-9]\")\n        .replace(\"[[:alpha:]]\", \"[a-zA-Z]\")\n        .replace(\"[[:digit:]]\", \"[0-9]\")\n        .replace(\"[[:lower:]]\", \"[a-z]\")\n        .replace(\"[[:upper:]]\", \"[A-Z]\")\n        .replace(\"[[:space:]]\", r\"[\\t\\n\\r ]\")\n        .replace(\"[[:print:]]\", r\"[\\x20-\\x7e]\")\n        .replace(\"[[:ascii:]]\", r\"[\\x00-\\x7f]\")\n}\n\n/// Calculate Shannon entropy of a string (base-2, over byte values).\n///\n/// Used to distinguish high-entropy secrets from low-entropy placeholders.\nfn shannon_entropy(s: &str) -> f64 {\n    if s.is_empty() {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n    let mut counts = [0u32; 256];\n    for byte in s.bytes() {\n        counts[byte as usize] += 1;\n    }\n    let len = s.len() as f64;\n    counts\n        .iter()\n        .filter(|&&c| c > 0)\n        .map(|&c| {\n            let p = c as f64 / len;\n            -p * p.log2()\n        })\n        .sum()\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Tests\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    fn make_sanitizer() -> Sanitizer {\n        Sanitizer::new(&[]).expect(\"failed to build sanitizer from vendored config\")\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn sanitizer_builds_from_vendored_toml() {\n        let s = make_sanitizer();\n        // Vendored config has 222 rules; allow for minor variation if updated.\n        assert!(\n            s.rule_count() >= 200,\n            \"expected >= 200 compiled rules, got {}\",\n            s.rule_count()\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn sanitizer_redacts_anthropic_api_key() {\n        let s = make_sanitizer();\n        // Anthropic API key format: sk-ant-api03-<93 chars>AA\n        let fake_key = format!(\n            \"sk-ant-api03-{:0>93}AA\",\n            \"ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789_-ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTU\"\n        );\n        let line = format!(\"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY={}\", fake_key);\n        let result = s.sanitize(&line);\n        assert!(\n            result.contains(\"[REDACTED:anthropic-api-key]\"),\n            \"expected redaction, got: {}\",\n            result\n        );\n        assert!(!result.contains(&fake_key), \"key should be redacted\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn sanitizer_redacts_gcp_api_key() {\n        let s = make_sanitizer();\n        // GCP API key: AIza + 35 alphanumeric/dash chars (high entropy)\n        let fake_key = \"AIzaSyBnFb9RkQ3mD2eWl8TpXa0vN7hJcK4oMiY\";\n        let line = format!(\"key = \\\"{}\\\"\", fake_key);\n        let result = s.sanitize(&line);\n        assert!(\n            result.contains(\"[REDACTED:gcp-api-key]\"),\n            \"expected gcp-api-key redaction, got: {}\",\n            result\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn sanitizer_preserves_bead_ids() {\n        let s = make_sanitizer();\n        // Bead IDs must never be redacted.\n        let line = \"processing bead needle-wysd.2.2 in workspace\";\n        let result = s.sanitize(line);\n        assert_eq!(result, line, \"bead ID should not be redacted\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn sanitizer_preserves_already_redacted() {\n        let s = make_sanitizer();\n        let line = \"token=[REDACTED:anthropic-api-key] was sanitized\";\n        let result = s.sanitize(line);\n        assert_eq!(result, line, \"already-redacted token should pass through\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn sanitizer_handles_low_entropy_placeholder() {\n        let s = make_sanitizer();\n        // A placeholder like \"AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA\" has zero entropy and should\n        // not be redacted even if it looks like an API key.\n        let line = \"AIzaAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA\";\n        let result = s.sanitize(line);\n        // Low entropy — should not be redacted.\n        assert!(\n            !result.contains(\"[REDACTED:\"),\n            \"low-entropy placeholder should not be redacted\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn sanitizer_with_custom_pattern() {\n        let custom = vec![CustomPattern {\n            id: \"test-key\".to_string(),\n            pattern: r\"(mykey-[a-f0-9]{32})\".to_string(),\n Clean up old traces based on retention policy.\n290\t///\n291\t/// - Failed beads (non-zero exit): delete after 30 days\n292\t/// - Successful beads (exit 0): prune data after 7 days, keep metadata only\n293\tpub fn cleanup_traces(\n294\t    traces_dir: &Path,\n295\t    retention_days_failed: u32,\n296\t    retention_days_success: u32,\n297\t) -> Result<TraceCleanupSummary> {\n298\t    let mut .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)\n306\t        .map(|d| d.as_secs())\n307\t        .unwrap_or(0);\n308\t\n309\t    // Iterate through bead trace directories.\n310\t    for entry in    Ok(e) => e,\n315\t            Err(_) => continue,\n316\t        };\n317\t\n318\t        let path = entry.path();\n319\t\n320\t        // Only process directories (bead-id subdirectories).\n321\t        if !path.is_dir() {\n322\t            continue;\n323\t        }\n324\t\n325\t        // Check metadata.json to determine outcome and age.\n326\t        let metadata_path = metadata\n336\t            .as_ref()\n337\t            .and_then(|m| now.checked_sub(m.captured_at.timestamp() as u64))\n338\t            .map(|secs| secs / 86400)\n339\t            .unwrap_or(u64::MAX);\n340\t\n341\t        let is_failed = metadata.as_ref().map(|m| m.exit_code != 0).unwrap_or(false);\n342\t        let is_pruned = metadata.as_ref().map(|m| This check is crucial for preventing infinite\n348\t        // loops where the same trace is counted repeatedly.\n349\t        let has_data_files = [\"trace.jsonl\", \"stdout.txt\", \"stderr.txt\"]\n350\t            .iter()\n351\t            .any(|file| path.join(file).exists());\n352\t\n353\t        let should_delete = is_failed && age_days > retention_days_failed as u64;\n354\t        // Only prune if metadata says not pruned AND data files actually exist.\n355\t        // If files are gone but metadata says not pruned, we have a partial\n356\t        // state from an interrupted run - fix the metadata and skip counting.\n357\t        let should_prune =\n358\t            !is_failed && !is_pruned && has_data_files && age_days > retention_days_success as u64;\n359\t\n360\t        // Fix up metadata for traces that were partially pruned (files gone\n361\t        // but metadata not updated). This is a recovery operation for interrupted pruning.\n402\tfn fix_pruned_metadata(trace_dir: &Path) -> Result<()> {\n403\t    let metadata_path = trace_dir.join(\"metadata.json\");\n404\t    if !metadata_path.exists() {\n405\t        return Ok(());\n406\t    }\n407\t\n408\t    let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&metadata_path)?;\n409\t    if let Ok(mut metadata) = serde_json::from_str::<TraceMetadata>(&content) {\n410\t        if !metadata.pruned {\n411\t            metadata.pruned = true;\n412\t            let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&metadata)?;\n413\t            std::fs::write(&metadata_path, json)?;\n414\t            tracing::debug!(\n415\t                path = %trace_dir.display(),\n416\t                \"fixed pruned metadata for partially-pruned trace\"\n417\t            );\n418\t        }\n419\t    }\n420\t    Ok(())\n421\t}\n422\t\n423\t/// Prune trace data files in a directory, keeping only metadata.json.\n424\t///\n425\t/// Updates metadata FIRST to mark as pruned, then removes data files.\n426\t/// This order is critical: if the process is interrupted after metadata\n427\t/// update but before file removal, the next cleanup will skip this trace\n428\t/// (because is_pruned=true) and only remove remaining files. This prevents\n429\t/// infinite loops where the same traces are counted as \"pruned\" repeatedly.\n430\tfn prune_trace_dir(trace_dir: &Path) -> Result<()> {\n431\t    // Step 1: Update metadata to mark as pruned BEFORE removing files.\n432\t    // This prevents the same trace from being counted as pruned multiple times\n433\t    // if the process is interrupted between metadata update and file removal.\n434\t    let metadata_path = trace_dir.join(\"metadata.json\");\n435\t    if metadata_path.exists() {\n436\t        let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&metadata_path)?;\n437\t        if let Ok(mut metadata) = serde_json::from_str::<TraceMetadata>(&content) {\n438\t            metadata.pruned = true;\n439\t            let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&metadata)?;\n440\t            std::fs::write(&metadata_path, json)?;\n441\t        }\n442\t    }\n443\t\n444\t    // Step 2: Remove trace data files after metadata is updated.\n445\t    // Use ? to propagate errors - if file removal fails, the operator should\n446\t    // know so they can investigate. Files that don't exist are skipped.\n447\t    for file in [\"trace.jsonl\", \"stdout.txt\", \"stderr.txt\"] {\n448\t        let path = trace_dir.join(file);\n449\t        if path.exists() {\n450\t            std::fs::remove_file(&path)\n451\t                .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to prune trace file: {}\", path.display()))?;\n452\t        }\n453\t    }\n454\t\n455\t    Ok(())\n456\t}\n457\t\n458\tuse std::time::SystemTime;\n459\t\n460\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n461\t// Tests\n462\t// }\n1011\t}\n1012\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"905126f8-8aaf-4aa9-bb79-105f79c52466","uuid":"64232d96-eb1d-45c6-ab17-4e1481f9c20d","timestamp":"2026-05-30T15:02:51.162Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/coding/NEEDLE/src/trace/mod.rs","content":"//! Trace capture: adapter-specific structured trace collection.\n//!\n//! This module captures full execution traces from agent runs including\n//! tool calls, agent reasoning, and verifier output. Traces are stored\n//! in `.beads/traces/<bead-id>/` with structured metadata.\n//!\n//! ## Trace Retention Policy\n//!\n//! - **Failed beads**: 30 days (full trace retained)\n//! - **Successful beads**: metadata-only after 7 days (trace data pruned)\n//!\n//! ## Directory Structure\n//!\n//! ```text\n//! .beads/traces/<bead-id>/\n//! ├── trace.jsonl     # Structured trace events (one JSON    /// Trace capture timestamp.\n    pub captured_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n    /// Adapter-specific trace format.\n    pub trace_format: TraceFormat,\n    /// Whether the trace data has been pruned (retention policy).\n    pub pruned: bool,\n    /// SHA-256 hex digest of the rendered prompt (identifies template version).\n    pub template_version: Option<String>,\n}\n\n/// Adapter-specific trace ClaudeJson,\n    /// OpenAI/Codex JSONL format.\n    OpenaiJsonl,\n    /// Aider markdown chat history.\n    AiderMarkdown,\n    /// Generic raw text capture.\n    RawText,\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Trace storage\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Manages trace storage for a bead execution.\npub struct TraceCapture {\n    /// Trace directory for this bead (`.beads/traces/<bead-id>`).\n    trace_dir: PathBuf,\n    /// Whether trace capture is enabled.\n    enabled: bool,\n    /// Optional sanitizer applied to all content before writing to disk.\n    sanitizer: Option<Arc<Sanitizer>>,\n}\n\nimpl TraceCapture {\n    /// Create a new trace capture for a bead without sanitization.\n    ///\n    /// `beads_root` is the workspace directory containing `.beads/`.\n    /// Returns `None` if trace capture is disabled.\n    pub fn new(bead_id: &BeadId, beads_root: &Path) -> Option<Self> {\n        Self::new_with_sanitizer(bead_id, beads_root, None)\n    }\n\n    /// Create a new trace capture for a bead with an optional sanitizer.\n    ///\n    /// When `sanitizer` is `Some`, all trace content is sanitized synchronously\n    /// before writing to disk (no unsanitized window on disk).\n    pub fn new_with_sanitizer(\n        bead_id: &BeadId,\n        beads_root: &Path,\n        sanitizer: Option<Arc<Sanitizer>>,\n    ) -> Option<Self> {\n        let trace_dir = beads_root\n            .join(\".beads\")\n            .join(\"traces\")\n            .join(bead_id.as_ref());\n\n        // Create the trace directory.\n        if let Err(e) = std::fs::create_dir_all(&trace_dir) {\n            tracing::warn!(\n                bead_id = %bead_id,\n                path = %trace_dir.display(),\n                error = %e,\n                \"failed to create trace directory, trace capture disabled\"\n            );\n            return None;\n        }\n\n        Some(TraceCapture {\n            trace_dir,\n            enabled: true,\n            sanitizer,\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Get the trace directory path.\n    pub fn trace_dir(&self) -> &Path {\n        &self.trace_dir\n    }\n\n    /// Write stdout to `stdout.txt`.\n    ///\n    /// Content is sanitized before writing if a sanitizer is configured.\n    pub fn write_stdout(&self, stdout: &str) -> Result<()> {\n        if !self.enabled {\n            return Ok(());\n        }\n        let content = self.sanitize(stdout);\n        let path = self.trace_dir.join(\"stdout.txt\");\n        std::fs::write(&path, content.as_bytes())\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to write stdout trace: {}\", path.display()))\n    }\n\n    /// Write stderr to `stderr.txt`.\n    ///\n    /// Content is sanitized before writing if a sanitizer is configured.\n    pub fn write_stderr(&self, stderr: &str) -> Result<()> {\n        if !self.enabled {\n            return Ok(());\n        }\n        let content = self.sanitize(stderr);\n        let path = self.trace_dir.join(\"stderr.txt\");\n        std::fs::write(&path, content.as_bytes())\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to write stderr trace: {}\", path.display()))\n    }\n\n    /// Write structured trace JSONL to `trace.jsonl`.\n    ///\n    /// Each line should be a valid JSON object. Lines are sanitized before\n    /// writing if a sanitizer is configured.\n    pub fn write_trace_jsonl(&self, trace_lines: &[String]) -> Result<()> {\n        if !self.enabled || trace_lines.is_empty() {\n            return Ok(());\n        }\n        let path = self.trace_dir.join(\"trace.jsonl\");\n        let joined = trace_lines.join(\"\\n\");\n        let content = self.sanitize(&joined);\n        std::fs::write(&path, content.as_bytes())\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to write trace JSONL: {}\", path.display()))\n    }\n\n    /// Sanitize text if a sanitizer is configured; otherwise return as-is.\n    fn sanitize<'a>(&self, text: &'a str) -> std::borrow::Cow<'a, str> {\n        match &self.sanitizer {\n            Some(s) => std::borrow::Cow::Owned(s.sanitize(text)),\n            None => std::borrow::Cow::Borrowed(text),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Write metadata to `metadata.json`.\n    pub fn write_metadata(&self, metadata: &TraceMetadata) -> Result<()> {\n        if !self.enabled {\n            return Ok(());\n        }\n        let path = self.trace_dir.join(\"metadata.json\");\n        let json =\n            serde_json::to_string_pretty(metadata).context(\"failed to serialize trace metadata\")?;\n        std::fs::write(&path, json)\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to write metadata: {}\", path.display()))\n    }\n\n    /// Finalize the trace and return the trace directory path.\n    ///\n    /// Returns `None` if trace capture was disabled.\n    pub fn finalize(self) -> Option<PathBuf> {\n        if self.enabled {\n            Some(self.trace_dir)\n        } else {\n            None\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Delete the entire trace directory.\n    pub fn delete(&self) -> Result<()> {\n        if self.trace_dir.exists() {\n            std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.trace_dir).with_context(|| {\n                format!(\n                    \"failed to delete trace /// Deletes trace.jsonl, stdout.txt, and stderr.txt, keeping only metadata.json.\n    /// Updates the `pruned` flag in metadata.\n    pub fn prune_trace_data(&self) -> Result<()> {\n        if !self.enabled {\n            return Ok(());\n        }\n\n        // Delete trace data files.\n        for file in [\"trace.jsonl\", \"stdout.txt\", \"stderr.txt\"] {\n            let path = self.trace_dir.join(file);\n            if path.exists() {\n                std::fs::remove_file(&path)\n                    .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to prune trace file: {}\", path.display()))?;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Update metadata to mark as pruned.\n        let metadata_path = self.trace_dir.join(\"metadata.json\");\n        if metadata_path.exists() {\n            let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&metadata_path)?;\n            if let Ok(mut metadata) = serde_json::from_str::<TraceMetadata>(&content) {\n                metadata.pruned = ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Trace format detection\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Detect trace format from agent adapter name.\npub fn detect_trace_format(agent_name: &str) -> TraceFormat {\n    match agent_name {\n        n if n.starts_with(\"claude-\") => TraceFormat::ClaudeJson,\n        n if n.contains(\"codex\") || n.contains(\"openai\") => TraceFormat::OpenaiJsonl,\n        n if n.contains(\"aider\") => TraceFormat::AiderMarkdown,\n        _ => TraceFormat::RawText,\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Trace retention cleanup\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Cleanup result for trace retention.\n#[derive(Debug, Default)]\npub struct TraceCleanupSummary {\n    /// Number of traces pruned (metadata kept).\n    pub traces_pruned: u32,\n    /// Number of traces fully deleted.\n    pub traces_deleted: u32,\n}\n\n/// Clean up old traces based on retention policy.\n///\n/// - Failed beads (non-zero exit): delete after 30 days\n/// - Successful beads (exit 0): prune data after 7 days, keep metadata only\npub fn cleanup_traces(\n    traces_dir: &Path,\n    retention_days_failed: u32,\n    retention_days_success: u32,\n) -> Result<TraceCleanupSummary> {\n    let mut summary = TraceCleanupSummary::default();\n\n    if !traces_dir.exists() {\n        return Ok(summary);\n    }\n\n    let now = SystemTime::now()\n        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)\n        .map(|d| d.as_secs())\n        .unwrap_or(0);\n\n    // Iterate through bead trace directories.\n    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(traces_dir)\n        .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to read traces directory: {}\", traces_dir.display()))?\n    {\n        let entry = match entry {\n            Ok(e) => e,\n            Err(_) => continue,\n        };\n\n        let path = entry.path();\n\n        // Only process directories (bead-id subdirectories).\n        if !path.is_dir() {\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check metadata.json to determine outcome and age.\n        let metadata_path = path.join(\"metadata.json\");\n        let metadata: Option<TraceMetadata> = metadata_path\n            .exists()\n            .then(|| {\n                let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&metadata_path).ok()?;\n                serde_json::from_str(&content).ok()\n            })\n            .flatten();\n\n        let age_days = metadata\n            .as_ref()\n            .and_then(|m| now.checked_sub(m.captured_at.timestamp() as u64))\n            .map(|secs| secs / 86400)\n            .unwrap_or(u64::MAX);\n\n        let is_failed = metadata.as_ref().map(|m| m.exit_code != 0).unwrap_or(false);\n        let is_pruned = metadata.as_ref().map(|m| m.pruned).unwrap_or(false);\n\n        // Check if trace data files actually exist before attempting to prune.\n        // This prevents counting a trace as \"pruned\" when the data files were\n        // already removed in a previous run but the metadata update failed\n        // or was interrupted. This check is crucial for preventing infinite\n        // loops where the same trace is counted repeatedly.\n        let has_data_files = [\"trace.jsonl\", \"stdout.txt\", \"stderr.txt\"]\n            .iter()\n            .any(|file| path.join(file).exists());\n\n        let should_delete = is_failed && age_days > retention_days_failed as u64;\n        // Only prune if metadata says not pruned AND data files actually exist.\n        // If files are gone but metadata says not pruned, we have a partial\n        // state from an interrupted run - fix the metadata and skip counting.\n        let should_prune =\n            !is_failed && !is_pruned && has_data_files && age_days > retention_days_success as u64;\n\n        // Fix up metadata for traces that were partially pruned (files gone\n        // but metadata not updated). This prevents infinite loops.\n        if !is_failed && !is_pruned && !has_data_files && age_days > retention_days_success as u64 {\n            if let Err(e) = fix_pruned_metadata(&path) {\n                tracing::debug!(\n                    path = %path.display(),\n                    error = %e,\n                    \"failed to fix pruned metadata for partially-pruned trace\"\n                );\n            }\n        }\n\n        if should_delete {\n            // Delete entire trace directory.\n            if let Err(e) = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&path) {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    path = %path.display(),\n                    error = %e,\n                    \"failed to delete old trace directory\"\n                );\n            } else {\n                summary.traces_deleted += 1;\n            }\n        } else if should_prune {\n            // Prune trace data, keep metadata.\n            if let Err(e) = prune_trace_dir(&path) {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    path = %path.display(),\n                    error = %e,\n                    \"failed to prune trace data\"\n                );\n            } else {\n                summary.traces_pruned += 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(summary)\n}\n\n/// Fix metadata for a trace that was partially pruned (data files gone but\n/// metadata not updated). This is a recovery operation for interrupted pruning.\nfn fix_pruned_metadata(trace_dir: &Path) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata_path = trace_dir.join(\"metadata.json\");\n    if !metadata_path.exists() {\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&metadata_path)?;\n    if let Ok(mut metadata) = serde_json::from_str::<TraceMetadata>(&content) {\n        if !metadata.pruned {\n            metadata.pruned = true;\n            let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&metadata)?;\n            std::fs::write(&metadata_path, json)?;\n            tracing::debug!(\n                path = %trace_dir.display(),\n                \"fixed pruned metadata for partially-pruned trace\"\n            );\n        }\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Prune trace data files in a directory, keeping only metadata.json.\n///\n/// Updates metadata FIRST to mark as pruned, then removes data files.\n/// This order is critical: if the process is interrupted after metadata\n/// update but before file removal, the next cleanup will skip this trace\n/// (because is_pruned=true) and only remove remaining files. This prevents\n/// infinite loops where the same traces are counted as \"pruned\" repeatedly.\nfn prune_trace_dir(trace_dir: &Path) -> Result<()> {\n    // Step 1: Update metadata to mark as pruned BEFORE removing files.\n    // This prevents the same trace from being counted as pruned multiple times\n    // if the process is interrupted between metadata update and file removal.\n    let metadata_path = trace_dir.join(\"metadata.json\");\n    if metadata_path.exists() {\n        let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&metadata_path)?;\n        if let Ok(mut metadata) = serde_json::from_str::<TraceMetadata>(&content) {\n            metadata.pruned = true;\n            let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&metadata)?;\n            std::fs::write(&metadata_path, json)?;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Step 2: Remove trace data files after metadata is updated.\n    // Use ? to propagate errors - if file removal fails, the operator should\n    // know so they can investigate. Files that don't exist are skipped.\n    for file in [\"trace.jsonl\", \"stdout.txt\", \"stderr.txt\"] {\n        let path = trace_dir.join(file);\n        if path.exists() {\n            std::fs::remove_file(&path)\n                .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to prune trace file: {}\", path.display()))?;\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nuse std::time::SystemTime;\n\n// median latency ({} ms) exceeds threshold ({} ms)\",\n        median, threshold\n    );\n}\n\n/// Specialized benchmark that reports median latency directly.\n///\n/// This complements the criterion
